- **memory-keeper**: Keeps your memory current with the least effort from you. Two triggers. (1) Capture - at the end of a turn or on "remember this", it notices a durable new fact and proposes a memory update. (2) Context - while reading a source (meeting, chat, email), it notices things named but unseen, and drift against what memory already says, and queues or surfaces them. Propose-only; it never writes to memory without your yes. Triggers on "remember this", "capture that", "what am I missing", "catch up your context", or automatically end-of-turn when confidence is high. With no branch argument it infers the parent via the fork point. Invoke with /merge [branch]. ([Darmikon/skills](https://github.com/Darmikon/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/git/merge))
- **merge-advisor**: Computes the ORDER in which a set of open PRs should be merged so that each one is still mergeable when its turn arrives. Measures pairwise file collisions, latent conflicts that only appear after an earlier PR lands, artifacts anchored to a base SHA that every other PR must regenerate, and CI queue capacity. Use when the user asks "in what order do I merge these", "which PR goes first", "how do I avoid conflicts merging all of these", "will merging this break the others", or has a backlog of open PRs against one base. Do NOT trigger for: reviewing a single PR, checking whether one branch is… ([DojoCodingLabs/make-no-mistakes-toolkit](https://github.com/DojoCodingLabs/make-no-mistakes-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/merge-advisor) · ★3)
- **merge-agent-work**: Merge Agent Branch ([stevengonsalvez/ainb-toolkit](https://github.com/stevengonsalvez/ainb-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/merge-agent-work) · ★1)
- **merge-and-cleanup**: Merge the current branch's PR (squash) and clean up — delete branch, switch to default branch, pull, remove worktree if applicable. Use when user says "/merge-and-cleanup", "merge and cleanup", or "merge this PR". ([ericboehs/agent-plugins](https://github.com/ericboehs/agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/git-utils/skills/merge-and-cleanup) · ★2)
- **merge-and-cleanup**: Squash merge a PR, delete the remote and local branch, remove the worktree (if working in one), and update main. Use after PR approval. ([juslee/aios](https://github.com/juslee/aios/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/merge-and-cleanup) · ★2)
- **merge-and-deploy**: Use when a human runs /merge-and-deploy to merge a PR and trigger or verify deployment. Don't use for tasks that require source changes or without explicit human confirmation at each gate. ([OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin](https://github.com/OutlineDriven/odin-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/odin-release/skills/merge-and-deploy) · ★38)
- **merge-and-doc**: Merge a PR, verify the merge, and update roadmap, changelog, or session documentation in one workflow. ([Dbochman/dotfiles](https://github.com/Dbochman/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/merge-and-doc) · ★2)
